encodedUri = UnsafeUriCharactersEncoder.encode(uri);\n        URI u = new URI(encodedUri);\n        String path;\n        if (u.getScheme() != null) {\n            // if there is a scheme then there is also a path\n            path = URISupport.extractRemainderPath(u, useRawUri());\n        } else {\n            // this uri has no context-path as the leading text is the component name (scheme)\n            path = null;\n        }\n\n        // use encoded or raw uri?\n        Map<String, Object> parameters;\n        if (useRawUri()) {\n            // when using raw uri then the query is taking Returns property configurer for endpoints\n\n**Key Properties:**\n- `autowiredEnabled` - Enables automatic autowiring of options\n- `bridgeErrorHandler` - Bridges consumer to Camel routing Error Handler\n- `lazyStartProducer` - Defers producer startup until first message\n\n---\n\n### 2. **DefaultEndpoint**\n**File Path:** `/workspace/core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultEndpoint.java`\n\n**Key Methods:**\n- `createAsyncProducer()` - Creates an AsyncProducer, handles lazy start mode\n- `createPollingConsumer()` - Creates EventDrivenPollingConsumer with configurable queue\n- `createExchange()` - Creates Exchange with default pattern\n- `createExchange(ExchangePattern pattern)` - Creates Exchange with specified pattern\n- `configureExchange(Exchange exchange)` - Hook for subclasses to configure exchanges\n- `setProperties(Object bean, Map<String, Object> parameters)` - Configures bean properties using PropertyBindingSupport\n- `configureConsumer(Consumer consumer)` - Configures consumer (CamelContext injection, error handler setup)\n- `configurePollingConsumer(PollingConsumer consumer)` - Configures polling consumer\n- `getId()` - Returns unique endpoint ID\n- `getEndpointUri()` - Returns endpoint URI (lazy initialized)\n- `getEndpointKey()` - Returns unique endpoint key (uri or path depending on lenient mode)\n\n**Key Properties:**\n- `endpointUri` - The endpoint URI\n- `exchangePattern` - Default exchange pattern (InOnly/InOut)\n- `autowiredEnabled` - Enables endpoint-level autowiring\n- `lazyStartProducer` - Defers producer startup\n- `bridgeErrorHandler` - Bridges consumer errors\n- `exceptionHandler` - Custom exception handler for consumers\n- `pollingConsumerQueueSize` - Queue size for EventDrivenPollingConsumer (default: 1000)\n- `pollingConsumerBlockWhenFull` - Whether to block when queue is full\n- `pollingConsumerBlockTimeout` - Timeout for blocking\n- `pollingConsumerCopy` - Whether to copy exchanges in polling consumer\n\n---\n\n### 3. **DefaultConsumer**\n**File Path:** `/workspace/core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultConsumer.java`\n\n**Key Methods:**\n- `createUoW(Exchange exchange)` - The endpoint this consumer belongs to\n- `processor` - The processor to handle exchanges\n- `asyncProcessor` - Async wrapper around processor\n- `exchangeFactory` - Per-consumer exchange factory\n- `exceptionHandler` - Handler for exceptions (default: LoggingExceptionHandler)\n- `route` - The route this consumer is part of\n- `routeId` - ID of the route\n\n**Message Processing Flow:**\n- Consumers use `defaultConsumerCallback()` to create async callbacks for processed exchanges\n- The callback handles exceptions and manages exchange release/cleanup\n- UnitOfWork is created and tracked per exchange for transactional support\n\n---\n\n### 4. **DefaultProducer**\n**File Path:** `/workspace/core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultProducer.java`\n\n**Key Methods:**\n- `getEndpoint()` - Returns the endpoint\n- `createExchange()` - Creates a new exchange from the endpoint\n- `isSingleton()` - Returns true if endpoint is singleton (delegates to endpoint)\n\n**Key Properties:**\n- `endpoint` - The endpoint this producer belongs to\n\n**Note:** This is an abstract base class. Subclasses must implement the `process(Exchange exchange)` method for actual message processing. The class provides basic infrastructure and lifecycle management (doStart/doStop).\n\n---\n\n### 5. **DefaultAsyncProducer**\n**File Path:** `/workspace/core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultAsyncProducer.java`\n\n**Key Methods:**\n- `process(Exchange exchange)` - Synchronous processing that uses AsyncProcessorAwaitManager to handle async operations\n- `processAsync(Exchange exchange)` - Asynchronous processing that returns CompletableFuture<Exchange>\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- Extends DefaultProducer and implements AsyncProducer interface\n- Converts synchronous process() calls to asynchronous operations using AsyncProcessorAwaitManager\n- Subclasses should override `process(Exchange exchange, AsyncCallback callback)` (inherited from AsyncProcessor) for actual async logic\n- Allows non-blocking message processing with callback-based patterns\n\n---\n\n### Architecture Summary\n\nThe routing hierarchy forms a pipeline:\n\n1. **DefaultComponent** creates and configures endpoints with parameters and handles URI parsing\n2. **DefaultEndpoint** manages exchange creation, consumer/producer factory methods, and polling consumer setup\n3. **DefaultConsumer** processes incoming messages, manages exchanges, unit of work, and exception handling\n4. **DefaultProducer** (or **DefaultAsyncProducer**) sends exchanges to external systems or next processor\n\nKey message exchange flow:\n- Consumer receives message → creates Exchange → wraps in UnitOfWork → calls async processor → callback releases resources\n- Producer receives Exchange → processes synchronously or asynchronously → sends result downstream\n- All layers support autowiring, lazy initialization, error handling, and property configuration"}],"totalDurationMs":30449,"totalTokens":57475,"totalToolUseCount":9,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5771,"cache_read_input_tokens":49868,"output_tokens":1832,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5771,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}}}

org.apache.camel.util.IOHelper;\nimport org.slf4j.Logger;\nimport org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;\n\npublic class RouteReifier extends ProcessorReifier<RouteDefinition> {\n\n    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(RouteReifier.class);\n\n    private static final String[] RESERVED_PROPERTIES = new String[] {\n            Route.ID_PROPERTY, Route.CUSTOM_ID_PROPERTY, Route.PARENT_PROPERTY,\n            Route.DESCRIPTION_PROPERTY, Route.NOTE_PROPERTY,\n            Route.GROUP_PROPERTY, Route.NODE_PREFIX_ID_PROPERTY,\n            Route.REST_PROPERTY, Route.CONFIGURATION_ID_PROPERTY };\n\n    public RouteReifier(CamelContext camelContext, ProcessorDefinition<?> definition) {\n        super(camelContext, (RouteDefinition) definition);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public Processor createProcessor() throws Exception {\n        throw new UnsupportedOperationException(\"Not implemented for RouteDefinition\");\n    }\n\n    public Route createRoute() {\n        try {\n            return doCreateRoute();\n        } catch (FailedToCreateRouteException e) {\n            throw e;\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            // location on route is stored on input\n            ProcessorDefinitionHelper.prepareSourceLocation(definition.getResource(), definition.getInput());\n            String source = LoggerHelper.getSourceLocationOnly(definition.getInput());\n            throw new FailedToCreateRouteException(definition.getRouteId(), source, definition.toString(), e);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Implementation methods\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    protected Route doCreateRoute() throws Exception {\n        // resolve endpoint\n        Endpoint endpoint = definition.getInput().getEndpoint();\n        if (endpoint == null) {\n            EndpointConsumerResolver def = definition.getInput().getEndpointConsumerBuilder();\n            if (def != null) {\n                endpoint = def.resolve(camelContext);\n            } else {\n                endpoint = resolveEndpoint(definition.getInput().getEndpointUri());\n            }\n        }\n\n        // create route\n        String id = definition.idOrCreate(camelContext.getCamelContextExtension().getContextPlugin(NodeIdFactory.class));\n        String desc = definition.getDescriptionText();\n        String note = definition.getNote();\n\n        Route route = PluginHelper.getRouteFactory(camelContext).createRoute(camelContext, definition, id,\n                desc, note, endpoint, definition.getResource());\n\n        // configure error handler\n        route.setErrorHandlerFactory(definition.getErrorHandlerFactory());\n\n        // configure variable\n        String variable = parseString(definition.getInput().getVariableReceive());\n        if (variable != null) {\n            // when using variable we need to turn on original message\n            route.setAllowUseOriginalMessage(true);\n        }\n\n        // configure tracing\n        if (definition.getTrace() != null) {\n            Boolean isTrace = parseBoolean(definition.getTrace());\n            if (isTrace != null) {\n                route.setTracing(isTrace);\n                if (isTrace) {\n                    LOG.debug(\"Tracing is enabled on route: {}\", definition.getId());\n                    // tracing is added in the DefaultChannel so we can enable\n                    // it on the fly\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // configure message history\n        if (definition.getMessageHistory() != null) {\n            Boolean isMessageHistory = parseBoolean(definition.getMessageHistory());\n            if (isMessageHistory != null) {\n                route.setMessageHistory(isMessageHistory);\n                if (isMessageHistory) {\n                    LOG.debug(\"Message history is enabled on route: {}\", definition.getId());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // configure Log EIP mask\n        if (definition.getLogMask() != null) {\n            Boolean isLogMask = parseBoolean(definition.getLogMask());\n            if (isLogMask != null) {\n                route.setLogMask(isLogMask);\n                if (isLogMask) {\n                    LOG.debug(\"Security mask for Logging is enabled on route: {}\", definition.getId());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // configure stream caching\n        if (definition.getStreamCache() != null) {\n            Boolean isStreamCache = parseBoolean(definition.getStreamCache());\n            if (isStreamCache != null) {\n                route.setStreamCaching(isStreamCache);\n                if (isStreamCache) {\n                    LOG.debug(\"StreamCaching is enabled on route: {}\", definition.getId());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // configure delayer\n        if (definition.getDelayer() != null) {\n            Long delayer = parseDuration(definition.getDelayer());\n            if (delayer != null) {\n                route.setDelayer(delayer);\n                if (delayer > 0) {\n                    LOG.debug(\"Delayer is enabled with: {} ms. on route: {}\", delayer, definition.getId());\n                } else {\n                    LOG.debug(\"Delayer is disabled on route: {}\", definition.getId());\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // configure auto startup\n        Boolean isAutoStartup = parseBoolean(definition.getAutoStartup());\n\n        // configure startup order\n        Integer startupOrder = definition.getStartupOrder();\n\n        // configure shutdown\n        if (definition.getShutdownRoute() != null) {\n            LOG.debug(\"Using ShutdownRoute {} on route: {}\", definition.getShutdownRoute(), definition.getId());\n            route.setShutdownRoute(parse(ShutdownRoute.class, definition.getShutdownRoute()));\n        }\n        if (definition.getShutdownRunningTask() != null) {\n            LOG.debug(\"Using ShutdownRunningTask {} on route: {}\", definition.getShutdownRunningTask(), definition.getId());\n            route.setShutdownRunningTask(parse(ShutdownRunningTask.class, definition.getShutdownRunningTask()));\n        }\n\n        // should inherit the intercept strategies we have defined\n        route.getInterceptStrategies().addAll(definition.getInterceptStrategies());\n\n        // notify route context created\n        for (LifecycleStrategy strategy : camelContext.getLifecycleStrategies()) {\n            strategy.onRouteContextCreate(route);\n        }\n\n        // validate route has output processors\n        if (!hasOutputs(definition.getOutputs(), true)) {\n            String at = definition.getInput().toString();\n            Exception cause = new IllegalArgumentException(\n                    \"Route \" + definition.getId() + \" has no output processors.\"\n                                                           + \" You need to add outputs to the route such as to(\\\"log:foo\\\").\");\n            // location on route is stored on input\n            ProcessorDefinitionHelper.prepareSourceLocation(definition.getResource(), definition.getInput());\n            String source = LoggerHelper.getSourceLocationOnly(definition.getInput());\n            throw new FailedToCreateRouteException(\n                    definition.getRouteId(), source, definition.toString(), at, cause);\n        }\n\n        List<ProcessorDefinition<?>> list = new ArrayList<>(definition.getOutputs());\n        for (ProcessorDefinition<?> output : list) {\n            try {\n                ProcessorReifier<?> reifier = ProcessorReifier.reifier(route, output);\n\n                // ensure node has id assigned\n                String outputId\n                        = output.idOrCreate(camelContext.getCamelContextExtension().getContextPlugin(NodeIdFactory.class));\n                String eip = reifier.getClass().getSimpleName().replace(\"Reifier\", \"\");\n                StartupStep step = camelContext.getCamelContextExtension().getStartupStepRecorder()\n                        .beginStep(ProcessorReifier.class, outputId, \"Create \" + eip + \" Processor\");\n\n                reifier.addRoutes();\n\n                camelContext.getCamelContextExtension().getStartupStepRecorder().endStep(step);\n            } catch (Exception e) {\n                // location on route is stored on input\n                String source = LoggerHelper\n                        .getLineNumberLoggerName(definition.getInput() != null ? definition.getInput().getLocation() : null);\n                throw new FailedToCreateRouteException(\n                        definition.getRouteId(), source, definition.toString(), output.toString(), e);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // now lets turn all the event driven consumer processors into a single route\n        List<Processor> eventDrivenProcessors = route.getEventDrivenProcessors();\n        if (eventDrivenProcessors.isEmpty()) {\n            return null;\n        }\n\n        // Set route properties\n        Map<String, Object> routeProperties = computeRouteProperties();\n\n        // always use a pipeline even if there are only 1 processor as the pipeline\n        // handles preparing the response from the exchange in regard to IN vs OUT messages etc\n        RoutePipeline target = new RoutePipeline(camelContext, eventDrivenProcessors);\n        target.setRouteId(id);\n\n        // and wrap it in a unit of work so the UoW is on the ProcessorReifier<? extends ProcessorDefinition<?>>> reifier\n                    = PROCESSORS.get(definition.getClass());\n            if (reifier != null) {\n                answer = EnrichReifier(route, definition);\n        } else if (definition instanceof FilterDefinition) {\n            return new FilterReifier(route, definition);\n        } else if (definition instanceof FinallyDefinition) {\n            return new FinallyReifier(route, definition);\n        } else if (definition instanceof IdempotentConsumerDefinition) {\n            return new IdempotentConsumerReifier(route, definition);\n        } else if (definition instanceof InterceptFromDefinition) {\n            return new InterceptFromReifier(route, definition);\n        } else if extends CamelContextAware, Service\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Endpoint.java` — Message Endpoint pattern interface; factory for Producer, Consumer, and Exchange instances\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Consumer.java` — Consumes message exchanges from an Endpoint; holds a reference to the processing Processor\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Producer.java` — Sends message exchanges to an Endpoint; extends both Processor and Service\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Processor.java` — Core @FunctionalInterface for processing Exchange instances; single method `process(Exchange)`\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Channel.java` — Routes exchanges between processors in the route graph; wraps processors with interceptors and error handlers\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Route.java` — Runtime route interface defining lifecycle, configuration, policies, and consumer/processor access\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/spi/InternalProcessor.java` — Internal processor API for cross-cutting concerns (UoW, tracing, debugging, JMX, route policies)\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/spi/CamelInternalProcessorAdvice.java` — Before/after advice interface for cross-cutting functionality in the routing engine\n\n### Base/Support Implementations (core/camel-support)\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultComponent.java` — Abstract base Component; implements URI parsing, parameter extraction, endpoint creation/configuration lifecycle\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultEndpoint.java` — Abstract base Endpoint; manages exchange creation, consumer/producer factory methods, polling consumer setup\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultConsumer.java` — Default Consumer implementation; manages UnitOfWork lifecycle, exchange factory, async processor delegation, exception handling\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultProducer.java` — Abstract base Producer; delegates exchange creation to endpoint\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultAsyncProducer.java` — Async-capable Producer; bridges sync process() to async via AsyncProcessorAwaitManager\n\n### Processor Chain (core/camel-core-processor)\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/Pipeline.java` — Chains processors sequentially; output of one becomes input to the next via PipelineTask/ReactiveExecutor\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/RoutePipeline.java` — Subclass of Pipeline used as the starting point for a Route\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/PipelineHelper.java` — Decides whether to continue processing based on exchange failure/rollback/stop state\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/SendProcessor.java` — Terminal processor that forwards an exchange to a destination Endpoint via its Producer\n\n### Engine (core/camel-base-engine)\n- `core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultRoute.java` — Default Route implementation; creates Consumer from Endpoint.createConsumer(processor), manages services lifecycle\n- `core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultChannel.java` — Default Channel implementation (extends CamelInternalProcessor); wires interceptors, error handlers, debugger, tracer, and JMX instrumentation around each processor node\n- `core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/CamelInternalProcessor.java` — Core internal processor executing CamelInternalProcessorAdvice before/after callbacks for UoW, route policy, inflight tracking, tracing, debugging, stream caching, message history\n\n### Model (core/camel-core-model)\n- `core/camel-core-model/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/model/RouteDefinition.java` — DSL model class for a route; holds FromDefinition (input), outputs (processor definitions), error handler, route policies, and configuration\n\n### Reifiers (core/camel-core-reifier)\n- `core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/RouteReifier.java` — Bridges RouteDefinition model to runtime Route; resolves endpoints, creates RoutePipeline, wraps with CamelInternalProcessor/UoW, configures route policies and advices\n- `core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/ProcessorReifier.java` — Abstract base reifier; converts ProcessorDefinition to Processor, wraps each in a Channel with interceptors and error handlers, builds composite Pipeline from outputs\n- `core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/SendReifier.java` — Converts ToDefinition to SendProcessor; resolves the destination Endpoint\n\n## Dependency Chain\n\n### Phase 1: Route Definition (Design Time / DSL)\n1. Entry point: `core/camel-core-model/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/model/RouteDefinition.java`\n   - User defines a route via Java DSL (`from(\"timer:tick\").to(\"log:out\")`) or XML\n   - `RouteDefinition` holds a `FromDefinition` (input endpoint) and a list of `ProcessorDefinition` outputs (each EIP step)\n\n### Phase 2: Route Reification (Startup / Model→Runtime Bridge)\n2. `RouteReifier.createRoute()` → `doCreateRoute()`\n   (`core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/RouteReifier.java`)\n   - Resolves the input `Endpoint` from the `FromDefinition`'s URI via `resolveEndpoint()`\n   - Creates a `DefaultRoute` via `PluginHelper.getRouteFactory()` with the resolved endpoint\n   - Configures route-level settings: error handler factory, tracing, message history, stream caching, delayer, auto-startup, shutdown policy\n\n3. For each output `ProcessorDefinition` in `RouteDefinition.getOutputs()`:\n   - `ProcessorReifier.reifier(route, output)` selects the appropriate reifier (e.g., `SendReifier` for `ToDefinition`)\n   - `reifier.addRoutes()` is called, which invokes `makeProcessor()`:\n     a. `createProcessor()` — calls the specific reifier's implementation (e.g., `SendReifier.createProcessor()` creates a `SendProcessor`)\n     b. `wrapProcessor(processor)` → `wrapChannel(processor, child)`:\n        - Creates a `DefaultChannel` via `PluginHelper.getInternalProcessorFactory().createChannel()`\n        - Collects interceptor strategies from: CamelContext → Route → Definition\n        - Calls `channel.initChannel()` which wires: JMX instrumentation, tracer, debugger, backlog tracer, message history advices\n        - Optionally wraps the channel output in an error handler via `wrapChannelInErrorHandler()`\n        - Calls `channel.postInitChannel()` to finalize wiring\n     c. The wrapped `Channel` is added to `route.getEventDrivenProcessors()`\n\n4. RouteReifier assembles the list of event-driven processors into a `RoutePipeline`\n   (`core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/RoutePipeline.java`)\n\n5. RouteReifier wraps the `RoutePipeline` in a `CamelInternalProcessor` with UnitOfWork advice:\n   - `PluginHelper.getInternalProcessorFactory().addUnitOfWorkProcessorAdvice(camelContext, target, route)`\n   - Adds route policy advice, inflight repository advice, JMX management advice, route lifecycle advice\n   - Adds REST binding advice, contract advice, variable advice as applicable\n   - Sets this `InternalProcessor` as `route.setProcessor(internal)`\n\n### Phase 3: Route Startup (Runtime)\n6. `DefaultRoute.gatherRootServices()` → `endpoint.createConsumer(processor)`\n   (`core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultRoute.java:685`)\n   - The route's endpoint creates a `Consumer` passing the route's processor (the `CamelInternalProcessor` wrapping the `RoutePipeline`)\n   - The consumer is started as part of the route's service lifecycle\n\n### Phase 4: Message Processing (Runtime)\n7. Consumer receives a message from the external source (e.g., timer fires, HTTP request arrives):\n   - `DefaultConsumer.createExchange(autoRelease)` creates a new `Exchange` from the `ExchangeFactory`\n   - Consumer calls `getAsyncProcessor().process(exchange, callback)` — this invokes the route's processor chain\n\n8. `CamelInternalProcessor.process(exchange, callback)` (top-level UoW wrapper):\n   (`core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/CamelInternalProcessor.java:285`)\n   - Executes all `CamelInternalProcessorAdvice.before(exchange)` in order:\n     - UnitOfWork creation/tracking\n     - Route policy `onExchangeBegin()`\n     - Inflight repository increment\n     - JMX instrumentation start\n     - Message history recording\n     - Stream caching\n   - Delegates to `processor.process(exchange, asyncCallback)` — the wrapped `RoutePipeline`\n   - After completion, executes all advices' `after(exchange, state)` in reverse order\n\n9. `Pipeline.process(exchange, callback)` (the RoutePipeline):\n   (`core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/Pipeline.java`)\n   - Acquires a `PipelineTask` from the task factory\n   - Schedules the task on the `ReactiveExecutor`\n   - `PipelineTask.run()` iterates through the processor list:\n     a. Calls `ExchangeHelper.prepareOutToIn(exchange)` to copy OUT→IN for the next step\n     b. Calls `PipelineHelper.continueProcessing(exchange)` to check for errors/stop/rollback\n     c. Gets the next `AsyncProcessor` (which is a `DefaultChannel` wrapping the actual EIP processor)\n     d. Calls `processor.process(exchange, this)` where `this` (PipelineTask) is the callback\n     e. On callback `done()`, re-schedules itself via `ReactiveExecutor` to process the next step\n     f. When all processors are exhausted, calls `ExchangeHelper.copyResults()` and signals the original callback\n\n10. `DefaultChannel.process(exchange, callback)` (each EIP step):\n    (`core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultChannel.java`)\n    - DefaultChannel extends `CamelInternalProcessor`, so it follows the same advice pattern:\n      - Executes per-node advices: message history, debugger, backlog tracer, JMX instrumentation\n      - Delegates to its output — which is either the error handler wrapping the next processor, or the next processor directly\n    - The error handler intercepts exceptions and applies redelivery/dead-letter-channel policies\n\n11. At a `to(\"...\")` step, the `SendProcessor.process(exchange, callback)` is invoked:\n    (`core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/SendProcessor.java`)\n    - Resolves and caches the destination's `AsyncProducer`\n    - Calls `producer.process(exchange, callback)` to deliver the exchange to the destination endpoint\n    - Restores the original exchange pattern after send\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Design Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Abstract Factory Pattern**: `Component` → `Endpoint` → `Consumer`/`Producer`. Each Component is a factory for Endpoints, and each Endpoint is a factory for Consumers and Producers. This three-level factory hierarchy enables pluggable transport/protocol support.\n\n2. **Pipes and Filters (Pipeline) Pattern**: The `Pipeline` processor implements the classic Pipes and Filters EIP. Each processor in the pipeline receives the output of the previous processor as its input. The `PipelineTask` manages sequential execution through the processor chain using a reactive callback model.\n\n3. **Chain of Responsibility Pattern**: The `Channel` (DefaultChannel) wraps each processor node with interceptors and error handlers, forming a chain of responsibility. Interceptor strategies are collected from three scopes: CamelContext-global, route-level, and definition-level.\n\n4. **Interceptor / Advice Pattern**: `CamelInternalProcessorAdvice` implements the before/after advice pattern for cross-cutting concerns. Multiple advices are composed into the `CamelInternalProcessor` and executed in sorted order (via `Ordered`), with `after()` callbacks running in reverse order. This replaces what would otherwise be deeply nested processor wrappers, reducing stack depth.\n\n5. **Bridge Pattern (Reifier)**: The `ProcessorReifier` hierarchy bridges the model layer (design-time DSL definitions) to the runtime layer (executable processors). Each `ProcessorDefinition` subclass has a corresponding `ProcessorReifier` that knows how to create the runtime processor. `RouteReifier` orchestrates this for the entire route.\n\n6. **Template Method Pattern**: `DefaultComponent.createEndpoint(String uri)` implements the template method — it handles URI parsing, parameter extraction, and property binding, then delegates to the abstract `createEndpoint(String uri, String remaining, Map<String, Object> parameters)` which concrete components override.\n\n7. **Decorator Pattern**: The `DefaultChannel` decorates the actual processor with error handling, interceptors, debugging, tracing, and JMX instrumentation. The `CamelInternalProcessor` at the route level decorates the entire `RoutePipeline` with UnitOfWork, route policy, and inflight tracking.\n\n8. **Object Pool Pattern**: Both `Pipeline` and `CamelInternalProcessor` use `PooledExchangeTaskFactory` and `PooledObjectFactory` respectively to reuse task objects, reducing GC pressure in high-throughput scenarios.\n\n### Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility |\n|-----------|---------------|\n| `Component` | URI-based factory for Endpoints; handles component-level configuration |\n| `Endpoint` | Factory for Consumers and Producers; defines exchange pattern; provides exchange creation |\n| `Consumer` | Receives messages from external source; creates Exchange; delegates to processor chain |\n| `Producer` | Sends Exchange to external destination; implements Processor interface |\n| `Processor` | Core processing unit; single `process(Exchange)` method |\n| `Pipeline` / `RoutePipeline` | Chains processors sequentially using reactive execution model |\n| `Channel` / `DefaultChannel` | Wraps individual processors with interceptors, error handlers, tracing, debugging |\n| `CamelInternalProcessor` | Executes cross-cutting advice (UoW, policy, JMX, tracing) before/after processor delegation |\n| `Route` / `DefaultRoute` | Runtime route; holds endpoint, consumer, processor chain, policies, configuration |\n| `RouteDefinition` | Design-time DSL model; holds from/to definitions and all EIP step definitions |\n| `RouteReifier` | Converts RouteDefinition → runtime Route + RoutePipeline + CamelInternalProcessor |\n| `ProcessorReifier` | Converts individual ProcessorDefinition → Processor, wrapped in Channel |\n| `SendReifier` / `SendProcessor` | Converts `to()` DSL to a processor that delegates to the destination's Producer |\n\n### Data Flow Description\n\nThe complete message routing flow through Apache Camel is:\n\n```\nExternal Source\n    ↓\nConsumer (receives message, creates Exchange)\n    ↓\nCamelInternalProcessor [route-level UoW wrapper]\n  ├── before: UnitOfWork, RoutePolicy, InflightRepository, JMX, Lifecycle\n  ↓\nRoutePipeline (iterates through processor list)\n  ↓\n  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │ For each EIP step:                           │\n  │                                              │\n  │  DefaultChannel [per-node CamelInternalProc] │\n  │    ├── before: MessageHistory, Debugger,     │\n  │    │          BacklogTracer, JMX             │\n  │    ↓                                         │\n  │  ErrorHandler (optional, wraps output)       │\n  │    ↓                                         │\n  │  Actual Processor (e.g., filter, transform)  │\n  │    ↓                                         │\n  │  DefaultChannel.after: reverse advices       │\n  │                                              │\n  │  ExchangeHelper.prepareOutToIn(exchange)     │\n  │  PipelineHelper.continueProcessing(exchange) │\n  └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n    ↓ (repeat for each processor in pipeline)\n\n  Final step often: SendProcessor\n    ↓\n  Producer.process(exchange) → destination Endpoint\n    ↓\nCamelInternalProcessor [route-level]\n  └── after: JMX, InflightRepository, RoutePolicy, UnitOfWork done\n    ↓\nConsumer callback → release Exchange\n```\n\n### Interface Contracts Between Components\n\n- **Component ↔ Endpoint**: Component creates Endpoints via `createEndpoint(uri, parameters)`. Endpoint receives its Component reference via `ComponentAware`.\n- **Endpoint ↔ Consumer**: Endpoint creates Consumer via `createConsumer(Processor)`. The Processor passed is the entire route processing chain (CamelInternalProcessor wrapping RoutePipeline).\n- **Endpoint ↔ Producer**: Endpoint creates Producer via `createProducer()` or `createAsyncProducer()`. Producer implements `Processor` so it can be used in the pipeline.\n- **Consumer ↔ Processor chain**: Consumer calls `getAsyncProcessor().process(exchange, callback)` to push the exchange into the route's processor chain.\n- **Pipeline ↔ Channel**: Pipeline holds a list of `AsyncProcessor` references (which are `DefaultChannel` instances). It calls each sequentially, using the reactive executor for scheduling.\n- **Channel ↔ ErrorHandler ↔ Processor**: Channel wraps the actual processor in an error handler (when `inheritErrorHandler` is true). The channel's `getOutput()` returns the error handler if present, otherwise the raw processor.\n- **ProcessorReifier ↔ ProcessorDefinition**: Each concrete reifier converts its corresponding definition to a runtime Processor, then `wrapChannel()` wraps it in a DefaultChannel with interceptors.\n- **RouteReifier ↔ RouteDefinition**: RouteReifier resolves endpoints, iterates output definitions, delegates to per-EIP reifiers, assembles the RoutePipeline, wraps with CamelInternalProcessor, and sets the result on DefaultRoute.\n\n## Summary\n\nApache Camel's message routing architecture follows a layered Abstract Factory pattern where **Component** creates **Endpoint**, which creates **Consumer** (inbound) and **Producer** (outbound), with **Processor** as the universal processing contract. The **RouteReifier** bridges the design-time DSL model (`RouteDefinition` with its `ProcessorDefinition` outputs) to the runtime by recursively converting each definition into a **Processor** wrapped in a **DefaultChannel** (which decorates it with interceptors, error handlers, and cross-cutting advice via `CamelInternalProcessorAdvice`), then assembling these channels into a **RoutePipeline** that chains them sequentially using a reactive execution model. At runtime, the Consumer receives messages, pushes them through a **CamelInternalProcessor** (handling UnitOfWork, route policies, JMX, and tracing) into the RoutePipeline, which iterates each Channel-wrapped processor step until reaching a terminal **SendProcessor** that delegates to the destination Endpoint's **Producer**.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":105,"cache_read_input_tokens":103279,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":105},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ca370d05-1d20-46f7-bd32-9d09a1f5f4fb","uuid":"968c880e-dfe4-46ea-8482-4d54a5598205"}

extends CamelContextAware, Service\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Endpoint.java` — Message Endpoint pattern interface; factory for Producer, Consumer, and Exchange instances\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Consumer.java` — Consumes message exchanges from an Endpoint; holds a reference to the processing Processor\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Producer.java` — Sends message exchanges to an Endpoint; extends both Processor and Service\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Processor.java` — Core @FunctionalInterface for processing Exchange instances; single method `process(Exchange)`\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Channel.java` — Routes exchanges between processors in the route graph; wraps processors with interceptors and error handlers\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/Route.java` — Runtime route interface defining lifecycle, configuration, policies, and consumer/processor access\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/spi/InternalProcessor.java` — Internal processor API for cross-cutting concerns (UoW, tracing, debugging, JMX, route policies)\n- `core/camel-api/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/spi/CamelInternalProcessorAdvice.java` — Before/after advice interface for cross-cutting functionality in the routing engine\n\n### Base/Support Implementations (core/camel-support)\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultComponent.java` — Abstract base Component; implements URI parsing, parameter extraction, endpoint creation/configuration lifecycle\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultEndpoint.java` — Abstract base Endpoint; manages exchange creation, consumer/producer factory methods, polling consumer setup\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultConsumer.java` — Default Consumer implementation; manages UnitOfWork lifecycle, exchange factory, async processor delegation, exception handling\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultProducer.java` — Abstract base Producer; delegates exchange creation to endpoint\n- `core/camel-support/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/support/DefaultAsyncProducer.java` — Async-capable Producer; bridges sync process() to async via AsyncProcessorAwaitManager\n\n### Processor Chain (core/camel-core-processor)\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/Pipeline.java` — Chains processors sequentially; output of one becomes input to the next via PipelineTask/ReactiveExecutor\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/RoutePipeline.java` — Subclass of Pipeline used as the starting point for a Route\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/PipelineHelper.java` — Decides whether to continue processing based on exchange failure/rollback/stop state\n- `core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/SendProcessor.java` — Terminal processor that forwards an exchange to a destination Endpoint via its Producer\n\n### Engine (core/camel-base-engine)\n- `core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultRoute.java` — Default Route implementation; creates Consumer from Endpoint.createConsumer(processor), manages services lifecycle\n- `core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultChannel.java` — Default Channel implementation (extends CamelInternalProcessor); wires interceptors, error handlers, debugger, tracer, and JMX instrumentation around each processor node\n- `core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/CamelInternalProcessor.java` — Core internal processor executing CamelInternalProcessorAdvice before/after callbacks for UoW, route policy, inflight tracking, tracing, debugging, stream caching, message history\n\n### Model (core/camel-core-model)\n- `core/camel-core-model/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/model/RouteDefinition.java` — DSL model class for a route; holds FromDefinition (input), outputs (processor definitions), error handler, route policies, and configuration\n\n### Reifiers (core/camel-core-reifier)\n- `core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/RouteReifier.java` — Bridges RouteDefinition model to runtime Route; resolves endpoints, creates RoutePipeline, wraps with CamelInternalProcessor/UoW, configures route policies and advices\n- `core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/ProcessorReifier.java` — Abstract base reifier; converts ProcessorDefinition to Processor, wraps each in a Channel with interceptors and error handlers, builds composite Pipeline from outputs\n- `core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/SendReifier.java` — Converts ToDefinition to SendProcessor; resolves the destination Endpoint\n\n## Dependency Chain\n\n### Phase 1: Route Definition (Design Time / DSL)\n1. Entry point: `core/camel-core-model/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/model/RouteDefinition.java`\n   - User defines a route via Java DSL (`from(\"timer:tick\").to(\"log:out\")`) or XML\n   - `RouteDefinition` holds a `FromDefinition` (input endpoint) and a list of `ProcessorDefinition` outputs (each EIP step)\n\n### Phase 2: Route Reification (Startup / Model→Runtime Bridge)\n2. `RouteReifier.createRoute()` → `doCreateRoute()`\n   (`core/camel-core-reifier/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/reifier/RouteReifier.java`)\n   - Resolves the input `Endpoint` from the `FromDefinition`'s URI via `resolveEndpoint()`\n   - Creates a `DefaultRoute` via `PluginHelper.getRouteFactory()` with the resolved endpoint\n   - Configures route-level settings: error handler factory, tracing, message history, stream caching, delayer, auto-startup, shutdown policy\n\n3. For each output `ProcessorDefinition` in `RouteDefinition.getOutputs()`:\n   - `ProcessorReifier.reifier(route, output)` selects the appropriate reifier (e.g., `SendReifier` for `ToDefinition`)\n   - `reifier.addRoutes()` is called, which invokes `makeProcessor()`:\n     a. `createProcessor()` — calls the specific reifier's implementation (e.g., `SendReifier.createProcessor()` creates a `SendProcessor`)\n     b. `wrapProcessor(processor)` → `wrapChannel(processor, child)`:\n        - Creates a `DefaultChannel` via `PluginHelper.getInternalProcessorFactory().createChannel()`\n        - Collects interceptor strategies from: CamelContext → Route → Definition\n        - Calls `channel.initChannel()` which wires: JMX instrumentation, tracer, debugger, backlog tracer, message history advices\n        - Optionally wraps the channel output in an error handler via `wrapChannelInErrorHandler()`\n        - Calls `channel.postInitChannel()` to finalize wiring\n     c. The wrapped `Channel` is added to `route.getEventDrivenProcessors()`\n\n4. RouteReifier assembles the list of event-driven processors into a `RoutePipeline`\n   (`core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/RoutePipeline.java`)\n\n5. RouteReifier wraps the `RoutePipeline` in a `CamelInternalProcessor` with UnitOfWork advice:\n   - `PluginHelper.getInternalProcessorFactory().addUnitOfWorkProcessorAdvice(camelContext, target, route)`\n   - Adds route policy advice, inflight repository advice, JMX management advice, route lifecycle advice\n   - Adds REST binding advice, contract advice, variable advice as applicable\n   - Sets this `InternalProcessor` as `route.setProcessor(internal)`\n\n### Phase 3: Route Startup (Runtime)\n6. `DefaultRoute.gatherRootServices()` → `endpoint.createConsumer(processor)`\n   (`core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultRoute.java:685`)\n   - The route's endpoint creates a `Consumer` passing the route's processor (the `CamelInternalProcessor` wrapping the `RoutePipeline`)\n   - The consumer is started as part of the route's service lifecycle\n\n### Phase 4: Message Processing (Runtime)\n7. Consumer receives a message from the external source (e.g., timer fires, HTTP request arrives):\n   - `DefaultConsumer.createExchange(autoRelease)` creates a new `Exchange` from the `ExchangeFactory`\n   - Consumer calls `getAsyncProcessor().process(exchange, callback)` — this invokes the route's processor chain\n\n8. `CamelInternalProcessor.process(exchange, callback)` (top-level UoW wrapper):\n   (`core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/CamelInternalProcessor.java:285`)\n   - Executes all `CamelInternalProcessorAdvice.before(exchange)` in order:\n     - UnitOfWork creation/tracking\n     - Route policy `onExchangeBegin()`\n     - Inflight repository increment\n     - JMX instrumentation start\n     - Message history recording\n     - Stream caching\n   - Delegates to `processor.process(exchange, asyncCallback)` — the wrapped `RoutePipeline`\n   - After completion, executes all advices' `after(exchange, state)` in reverse order\n\n9. `Pipeline.process(exchange, callback)` (the RoutePipeline):\n   (`core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/Pipeline.java`)\n   - Acquires a `PipelineTask` from the task factory\n   - Schedules the task on the `ReactiveExecutor`\n   - `PipelineTask.run()` iterates through the processor list:\n     a. Calls `ExchangeHelper.prepareOutToIn(exchange)` to copy OUT→IN for the next step\n     b. Calls `PipelineHelper.continueProcessing(exchange)` to check for errors/stop/rollback\n     c. Gets the next `AsyncProcessor` (which is a `DefaultChannel` wrapping the actual EIP processor)\n     d. Calls `processor.process(exchange, this)` where `this` (PipelineTask) is the callback\n     e. On callback `done()`, re-schedules itself via `ReactiveExecutor` to process the next step\n     f. When all processors are exhausted, calls `ExchangeHelper.copyResults()` and signals the original callback\n\n10. `DefaultChannel.process(exchange, callback)` (each EIP step):\n    (`core/camel-base-engine/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/impl/engine/DefaultChannel.java`)\n    - DefaultChannel extends `CamelInternalProcessor`, so it follows the same advice pattern:\n      - Executes per-node advices: message history, debugger, backlog tracer, JMX instrumentation\n      - Delegates to its output — which is either the error handler wrapping the next processor, or the next processor directly\n    - The error handler intercepts exceptions and applies redelivery/dead-letter-channel policies\n\n11. At a `to(\"...\")` step, the `SendProcessor.process(exchange, callback)` is invoked:\n    (`core/camel-core-processor/src/main/java/org/apache/camel/processor/SendProcessor.java`)\n    - Resolves and caches the destination's `AsyncProducer`\n    - Calls `producer.process(exchange, callback)` to deliver the exchange to the destination endpoint\n    - Restores the original exchange pattern after send\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Design Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Abstract Factory Pattern**: `Component` → `Endpoint` → `Consumer`/`Producer`. Each Component is a factory for Endpoints, and each Endpoint is a factory for Consumers and Producers. This three-level factory hierarchy enables pluggable transport/protocol support.\n\n2. **Pipes and Filters (Pipeline) Pattern**: The `Pipeline` processor implements the classic Pipes and Filters EIP. Each processor in the pipeline receives the output of the previous processor as its input. The `PipelineTask` manages sequential execution through the processor chain using a reactive callback model.\n\n3. **Chain of Responsibility Pattern**: The `Channel` (DefaultChannel) wraps each processor node with interceptors and error handlers, forming a chain of responsibility. Interceptor strategies are collected from three scopes: CamelContext-global, route-level, and definition-level.\n\n4. **Interceptor / Advice Pattern**: `CamelInternalProcessorAdvice` implements the before/after advice pattern for cross-cutting concerns. Multiple advices are composed into the `CamelInternalProcessor` and executed in sorted order (via `Ordered`), with `after()` callbacks running in reverse order. This replaces what would otherwise be deeply nested processor wrappers, reducing stack depth.\n\n5. **Bridge Pattern (Reifier)**: The `ProcessorReifier` hierarchy bridges the model layer (design-time DSL definitions) to the runtime layer (executable processors). Each `ProcessorDefinition` subclass has a corresponding `ProcessorReifier` that knows how to create the runtime processor. `RouteReifier` orchestrates this for the entire route.\n\n6. **Template Method Pattern**: `DefaultComponent.createEndpoint(String uri)` implements the template method — it handles URI parsing, parameter extraction, and property binding, then delegates to the abstract `createEndpoint(String uri, String remaining, Map<String, Object> parameters)` which concrete components override.\n\n7. **Decorator Pattern**: The `DefaultChannel` decorates the actual processor with error handling, interceptors, debugging, tracing, and JMX instrumentation. The `CamelInternalProcessor` at the route level decorates the entire `RoutePipeline` with UnitOfWork, route policy, and inflight tracking.\n\n8. **Object Pool Pattern**: Both `Pipeline` and `CamelInternalProcessor` use `PooledExchangeTaskFactory` and `PooledObjectFactory` respectively to reuse task objects, reducing GC pressure in high-throughput scenarios.\n\n### Component Responsibilities\n\n| Component | Responsibility |\n|-----------|---------------|\n| `Component` | URI-based factory for Endpoints; handles component-level configuration |\n| `Endpoint` | Factory for Consumers and Producers; defines exchange pattern; provides exchange creation |\n| `Consumer` | Receives messages from external source; creates Exchange; delegates to processor chain |\n| `Producer` | Sends Exchange to external destination; implements Processor interface |\n| `Processor` | Core processing unit; single `process(Exchange)` method |\n| `Pipeline` / `RoutePipeline` | Chains processors sequentially using reactive execution model |\n| `Channel` / `DefaultChannel` | Wraps individual processors with interceptors, error handlers, tracing, debugging |\n| `CamelInternalProcessor` | Executes cross-cutting advice (UoW, policy, JMX, tracing) before/after processor delegation |\n| `Route` / `DefaultRoute` | Runtime route; holds endpoint, consumer, processor chain, policies, configuration |\n| `RouteDefinition` | Design-time DSL model; holds from/to definitions and all EIP step definitions |\n| `RouteReifier` | Converts RouteDefinition → runtime Route + RoutePipeline + CamelInternalProcessor |\n| `ProcessorReifier` | Converts individual ProcessorDefinition → Processor, wrapped in Channel |\n| `SendReifier` / `SendProcessor` | Converts `to()` DSL to a processor that delegates to the destination's Producer |\n\n### Data Flow Description\n\nThe complete message routing flow through Apache Camel is:\n\n```\nExternal Source\n    ↓\nConsumer (receives message, creates Exchange)\n    ↓\nCamelInternalProcessor [route-level UoW wrapper]\n  ├── before: UnitOfWork, RoutePolicy, InflightRepository, JMX, Lifecycle\n  ↓\nRoutePipeline (iterates through processor list)\n  ↓\n  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n  │ For each EIP step:                           │\n  │                                              │\n  │  DefaultChannel [per-node CamelInternalProc] │\n  │    ├── before: MessageHistory, Debugger,     │\n  │    │          BacklogTracer, JMX             │\n  │    ↓                                         │\n  │  ErrorHandler (optional, wraps output)       │\n  │    ↓                                         │\n  │  Actual Processor (e.g., filter, transform)  │\n  │    ↓                                         │\n  │  DefaultChannel.after: reverse advices       │\n  │                                              │\n  │  ExchangeHelper.prepareOutToIn(exchange)     │\n  │  PipelineHelper.continueProcessing(exchange) │\n  └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n    ↓ (repeat for each processor in pipeline)\n\n  Final step often: SendProcessor\n    ↓\n  Producer.process(exchange) → destination Endpoint\n    ↓\nCamelInternalProcessor [route-level]\n  └── after: JMX, InflightRepository, RoutePolicy, UnitOfWork done\n    ↓\nConsumer callback → release Exchange\n```\n\n### Interface Contracts Between Components\n\n- **Component ↔ Endpoint**: Component creates Endpoints via `createEndpoint(uri, parameters)`. Endpoint receives its Component reference via `ComponentAware`.\n- **Endpoint ↔ Consumer**: Endpoint creates Consumer via `createConsumer(Processor)`. The Processor passed is the entire route processing chain (CamelInternalProcessor wrapping RoutePipeline).\n- **Endpoint ↔ Producer**: Endpoint creates Producer via `createProducer()` or `createAsyncProducer()`. Producer implements `Processor` so it can be used in the pipeline.\n- **Consumer ↔ Processor chain**: Consumer calls `getAsyncProcessor().process(exchange, callback)` to push the exchange into the route's processor chain.\n- **Pipeline ↔ Channel**: Pipeline holds a list of `AsyncProcessor` references (which are `DefaultChannel` instances). It calls each sequentially, using the reactive executor for scheduling.\n- **Channel ↔ ErrorHandler ↔ Processor**: Channel wraps the actual processor in an error handler (when `inheritErrorHandler` is true). The channel's `getOutput()` returns the error handler if present, otherwise the raw processor.\n- **ProcessorReifier ↔ ProcessorDefinition**: Each concrete reifier converts its corresponding definition to a runtime Processor, then `wrapChannel()` wraps it in a DefaultChannel with interceptors.\n- **RouteReifier ↔ RouteDefinition**: RouteReifier resolves endpoints, iterates output definitions, delegates to per-EIP reifiers, assembles the RoutePipeline, wraps with CamelInternalProcessor, and sets the result on DefaultRoute.\n\n## Summary\n\nApache Camel's message routing architecture follows a layered Abstract Factory pattern where **Component** creates **Endpoint**, which creates **Consumer** (inbound) and **Producer** (outbound), with **Processor** as the universal processing contract. The **RouteReifier** bridges the design-time DSL model (`RouteDefinition` with its `ProcessorDefinition` outputs) to the runtime by recursively converting each definition into a **Processor** wrapped in a **DefaultChannel** (which decorates it with interceptors, error handlers, and cross-cutting advice via `CamelInternalProcessorAdvice`), then assembling these channels into a **RoutePipeline** that chains them sequentially using a reactive execution model. At runtime, the Consumer receives messages, pushes them through a **CamelInternalProcessor** (handling UnitOfWork, route policies, JMX, and tracing) into the RoutePipeline, which iterates each Channel-wrapped processor step until reaching a terminal **SendProcessor** that delegates to the destination Endpoint's **Producer**.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
